# ZenEng v129: the last port on songs 40–45 — test report

*Words and music of all five songs: Jason Bryant. The spec is ZenEng v129 (Module 27.73). The session's 500–518 were ported once, with no new aims (521), onto Run 3's code of record: songs 40, 42 and 43 on their 438 charts (Run 3's Part 2), songs 44 and 45 on their own (Run 3's Part 1). All five songs were rerun, main and control, each made twice at the seeds of record, and every check is read on both draws (479). The paper was not edited. New findings are numbered from 530. This is the last report on these songs.*

## What was run

- **The port** (ZE_PART=1): 500, 501, 502, 503, 504, 505, 508, 513, 519 and 520, ported once with no new aim (521).
  - Songs 40, 42 and 43 stay on their 438 charts (Run 3's Part 2).
  - Songs 44 and 45 stay on their own charts (Run 3's Part 1).
  - All five songs were run, 1,000 builds main and control, each made twice at the seeds of record.
- **The check:** ZE_PART=0 from the final files reproduces Run 3 (song 43 main).
- **A side run:** song 45, main, with 501 off (its joins before a line).
- **The bass (516, 517):** the records of songs 40, 43 and 44 were rebuilt again with bass_v129.py, read beside the hand-written bass and Run 3's routine.
- **Readings,** each on main and control:
  - every test (readings_v129.py);
  - separation on the agreed 30 (sep_v129.py), Run 3 read the same way;
  - Window 147's measures with the engine's window (reread_v129.py, 519);
  - the kept pass rates (kept_pass_v129.py).
- **The comparison:** Run 3's runs of record (songs 40, 42, 43: Part 2; 44, 45: Part 1).

## How each fix was ported

Every fix is a whole-statement patch in gen_v129.py, gated by ON9() at ZE_PART=1. ZE_PART=0 reproduces Run 3: song 43 main, 9fab8a9e1e46, 872 survivors, matched.

- **500.** 465's class share is the product of the kept share for the line's notes and the kept share for its slot's beats (one bar 4, two bars 8, longer 12–16), over the kept classes' overall share. A class with a share that the slot cannot reach is counted by notes and slot.
- **501.** 414's seconds bound is off. For half its tries, a drawn line's onsets a beat between its first and last onset must lie in the kept 25th–75th for its notes (zeneng_v128_kept_lines.json). After that the bound is given up and counted.
- **502.** Song 45's chorus floor is the verse's median plus three, capped at the chorus top less three (Run 2's Part 2).
- **503.** One knob, ctb503, weighs a chord tone on the beat in the sing-again walk. It is aimed on unaimed builds at verse 1's chord tones on the beat in the same builds (the engine's windows).
- **504.** 452's band knobs are keyed by the line's own notes a bar: its syllables per slot bar times the song's notes to syllables in the judged windows, read on unaimed builds. The zigzag knob takes the band of the line being walked.
- **505.** 389's rate is held at one. The run weight (490) alone is aimed, on every section of the band, with this round's unaimed builds and the last round's pooled (60).
- **508.** A return or sung-again line that follows a designed line known ahead (song 45's FIXR) is fitted with the breath that line's last onset needs (GAP_MIN) in place of the open −12.
  - This is where song 45's joins before a copy died: the last chorus's first line is designed, and the return after it took a pickup from its source.
- **513.** After the inversions are drawn, each is tested on the finished chart. One with no step to a neighbouring change is taken back, and the test is repeated.
- **519.** The content judge of record is zeneng_v129_judge.json. The share of complete builds under the kept songs' 5th percentile (−0.72) is printed; 254's floor stays on the v108 judge.
- **520.** 455 is withdrawn (its weight was already one under 490). 408's width knob is gone from the knobs and the record, and S16B is out of the record. Turns stay with 400's knob, and 468 is kept. The window's range is printed beside each song's design and not aimed.
- **512.** A test that no kept song passes read as a build is read (0% in kept_pass_v129.json) is shown as a reading of the mean of builds, not as held or not held.
- **516.** 448's 35–59% of notes on a kick is read on the bars that have a kick.
- **517.** The bass's eight bars are A B A C A B D E: a third cell, D, in the second four bars, and its fill, E. The record's seed is new ("… v129").
- **518.** Not ported to these songs: their picks stand on the charts they were drawn on. It applies to the next song (522).
- **The reread (519).** Window 147's measures are kept for comparison with the earlier runs, but its window is now the engine's: every note sung in the eight bars, the chord at the note. Run 3 is read again the same way.

## The table of tests: Song 43, Blue Movie

Held in both the main run and the control: 55 of 66 with a test that a kept song can pass. Not held: 347, 368, 389, 394, 424, 458, 490, 500, 501, 505, 405. ## Findings (530–)

### 530. 503's knob moves verse 2 toward verse 1, but not far enough, and the paired test falls back.

**Problem:** The sing-again walk's chord-tone knob came down to 0.56–0.91 and stopped there; the calibration ends before it reaches verse 1's rate. Verse 2 still carries more chord tones on the beat than verse 1 on every song.

**Evidence** (engine windows, verse 1 / verse 2, main; knob):

| Song | Verse 1 / verse 2 | Knob |
|---|---|---|
| 40 | 0.721 / 0.729 | 0.83 |
| 42 | 0.632 / 0.669 | 0.56 |
| 43 | 0.669 / 0.694 | 0.72 |
| 44 | 0.669 / 0.741 | 0.57 |
| 45 | 0.669 / 0.699 | 0.91 |

- The paired test (Window 147's measures, the engine's window): 20 / 20 at Run 3, 18 / 19 now.
- Pitch kept: 0.763 / 0.757 → 0.755 / 0.762.

**Fix:** For the next song, let 503's knob take its full step every round until verse 2's rate is within two points (it is the one knob still short of its figure).

**Check:** Verse 2's chord tones on the beat within two points of verse 1's on the next song, and the paired test at 20 of 27 or more.

### 531. 501 holds on the songs near the kept tempo, and costs the slow songs their joins.

**Problem:** Read in onsets a beat, the bound is met on songs 40 and 44. On songs 42 and 45 the lines are drawn denser (9–12 notes at 1.78–1.82 a beat, 13+ at 2.0–2.36), and song 43's 3–5-note lines are one designed figure at 0.62 a beat. Holding the bound for half the tries redraws heavily, and on song 45 it leaves too little breath before the next line.

**Evidence:**
- Redraws: song 40 22,488; song 45 49,166.
- 465 given up: song 40 7.4% → 21%, song 45 11% → 24%.
- Song 45, joins before a line: 31 / 14 → 71 / 78.
- Song 45's side run with 501 off: joins 25, but align deaths 46; survivors 851 against 824.

**Fix:** For the next song, read 501 on the kept 5th–95th (not the middle half), so that it bounds the line without redrawing most of them.

**Check:** On the next song, 501 given up under one line in twenty, with joins before a line within two spreads of the run without it.

### 532. 500's class by notes and slot brings two-bar slots near the kept shares; one-bar slots cannot reach them.

**Problem:** Combined with the slot, the kept share for a one-bar slot puts class 0 at about half of lines. A one-bar slot reaches class 0 only through the next line's pickup.

**Evidence** (class 0 / 1 / 2, drawn against kept):

| Song, line | Drawn | Kept |
|---|---|---|
| Song 40, 6–8 notes, two-bar slot | 0.161 / 0.773 / 0.066 | 0.209 / 0.712 / 0.079 |
| Song 44, 6–8 notes, two-bar slot | 0.295 / 0.660 / 0.045 | 0.209 / 0.712 / 0.079 |
| Song 40, 6–8 notes, one-bar slot | 0.02 / 0.98 / 0 | 0.50 / 0.49 / 0 |

- Classes out of a slot's reach were counted 3,491 (song 44) to 13,675 times (song 40), most of them class 2 for 9–12 syllables in a two-bar slot.
- Empty bars: 0.182 / 0.155 → 0.148 / 0.141, not a real change (−0.024 against 0.054).

**Fix:** For the next song, write the slots in the form so that kept classes are reachable: a line of nine or more syllables in a slot of three bars or more, if the lyric allows.

**Check:** On the next song, each class within ten points of the kept share for its notes and slot.

### 533. 502 and 508 do what they were for: song 45 keeps its lift and its joins before a copy.

**Problem:** None; a reading.

**Evidence** (song 45, Run 3 → v129, main / control):

| Reading | Run 3 | v129 |
|---|---|---|
| Design deaths | 160 / 217 | 17 / 12 |
| Joins before a copy | 53 / 38 | 0 / 0 |
| Survivors | 614 / 633 | 824 / 814 |

- The return after the designed first line of the last chorus was fitted to its breath 899 times.

**Fix:** None.

**Check:** Printed.

### 534. 504 and 505 leave the dense windows and the runs where they were.

**Problem:** Keyed by the line's own notes a bar (notes to syllables 1.01–1.03), the band knobs still meet the dense windows rarely. With 389's rate at one, every line may run, and the run weight falls to 0.05–0.34 to hold runs down. It overshoots on some bands and not others.

**Evidence:**
- Equal gaps at 5.5–9 notes a bar: 0.525 / 0.492 → 0.519 / 0.460 (kept 0.435).
- 405 holds on both draws on no song (42's control and 43's main hold).
- Song 40's 5.5+ band run weight is 0.047.

**Fix:** None for these songs. The next song is read on the same knobs and is the test of whether they carry.

**Check:** On the next song, drawable runs within ten points on bands of 200 onsets or more.

### 535. 513 takes back the inversion that had lost its step; 440 holds on every chart.

**Problem:** None; a reading.

**Evidence:**
- Song 43's Dm/A was taken back; G/D and Em/B stand, each with a step.
- Song 42's four inversions all have steps.

**Fix:** None.

**Check:** Printed.

### 536. The v129 judge reads the builds as the paper does.

**Problem:** None; 519's reading.

**Evidence:** Complete builds under the kept songs' 5th percentile (−0.72), main / control:

| Song | v129 | The paper's figure (Run 3) |
|---|---|---|
| 40 | 57% / 56% | 51% |
| 42 | 60% / 60% | 66% |
| 43 | 43% / 47% | 47% |
| 44 | 86% / 91% | 88% |
| 45 | 88% / 96% | 96% |

- The floor stays on the v108 judge.

**Fix:** None.

**Check:** Printed.

### 537. Separation does not move, and its two draws now differ by 0.034.

**Problem:** None; 479's reading.

**Evidence:**
- Songs 40, 42 and 43: 0.739 / 0.734 → 0.756 / 0.722, a change of +0.003 against twice the difference, 0.068.
- Five songs: 0.778 / 0.796 → 0.769 / 0.768.
- The one real change on Window 147's measures is turns: 0.622 / 0.619 → 0.634 / 0.631 (twice the difference 0.007), away from the kept 0.584–0.594, with 408 withdrawn.

**Fix:** None.

**Check:** Printed.

### 538. 516 and 517 lift the bass's windows, except where the drums leave every eighth to the kick.

**Problem:** The third cell gives song 40 four bar rhythms in eight bars, the pop-and-rock median and more. Songs 43 and 44 stay at two: with four kicks a bar, the density needed to keep under 59% of notes on a kick fills every eighth, so every cell has the same rhythm.

**Evidence:**
- Windows passing every test (hand-written → Run 3 → v129):

| Song | Hand-written | Run 3 | v129 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 40 | 0 of 15 | 13 of 16 | 14 of 16 |
| 43 | 0 of 12 | 11 of 13 | 13 of 13 |
| 44 | 0 of 13 | 12 of 13 | 13 of 13 |

- Song 40's two failures: its intro (31% of notes on kicked bars) and its second chorus (a step into the next bar in 71% of changes).

**Fix:** For the next song, if it has four kicks a bar, vary the cells by pitch and articulation where the rhythm cannot vary.

**Check:** Two bar rhythms or more in eight bars on a four-on-the-floor song.

### 539. The range beside each song's design.

**Problem:** None; 520's reading.

**Evidence** (window range, verse / chorus, main; kept 10.9):

| Song | Verse / chorus | The design |
|---|---|---|
| 40 | 9.65 / 10.19 | No designed range |
| 42 | 11.06 / 10.45 | No designed range |
| 43 | 10.33 / 10.70 | No designed range |
| 44 | 6.32 / 9.61 | The verse's 6.3 semitones |
| 45 | 6.88 / 6.31 | The lift |

**Fix:** None.

**Check:** Printed.

## For the next song (522)

- The default path, drawn on a 438 chart (440's step tested on the finished chart).
